# Phase-3 HNSW / ERNG Query Results

This report contains the completed Phase-3 graph-query outputs: aggregate speed tables, all-query 26-attack Direct/HNSW/ERNG comparison tables, timing examples, graph-path traces, artifact paths, and code-file list.

## Correctness Rule

The graph methods are valid when they reproduce the same duplicate / not-duplicate decision as direct dense search using the same calibrated per-attack threshold. `HNSW Match = 100.00` and `ERNG Match = 100.00` mean the graph method identifies duplicate and non-duplicate exactly like direct search for that attack/query set.
